Run log excerpt (refresh-copies), redacted for this repository.
Standing test spot, main 1ef9e073 + diagnostics counters (unpublished branch refresh-count, 7a27b36b),
-RX7900XT (now -Tuned), VICTORY_CPU_STATS=1. Line 'copies of the refreshes in front, why, per frame'
says why pages are taken again; 'look at landed pages' is the echo of our own landings (about half).
Machine: AMD Radeon RX 7900 XTX 24 GB, AMD Ryzen 9 5900X, 32 GB RAM, Windows 11 Pro.
Redacted: only the layer's own counter lines are kept (never the title's output); paths, the host
name, the title's id, name, files and build hash, and console names are replaced by <...> placeholders.
Times are UTC, as in the original log.
Window: 09:07:53-09:10:06 UTC; lines kept: the counters this topic needs.
Thinned: at most one line of each kind every 5 s (A/B phases, frame-time blocks and long frames all kept).
